User

podrias generarme un fichero cowsia.html que mostrase las funcionalidades y usos de CowsIA (app desplegada aqui) con captura de pantalla y menu de funcionalidades ?

Assistant

Voy a revisar la aplicación desplegada y su estructura para identificar las funcionalidades reales. Después prepararé cowsia.html con un menú navegable y capturas auténticas de la app, reutilizando sus estilos cuando sea conveniente.

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { createRoot } from "react-dom/client";
import {
  BarChart3,
  CalendarPlus,
  ClipboardList,
  Download,
  ExternalLink,
  FileUp,
  Gauge,
  Image,
  Images,
  LockKeyhole,
  LogOut,
  Map as MapIcon,
  MapPin,
  Plus,
  RefreshCcw,
  Search,
  Trash2,
  UserPlus,
  X,
} from "lucide-react";
import L from "leaflet";
import { MapContainer, Marker, Popup, TileLayer } from "react-leaflet";
import "leaflet/dist/leaflet.css";
import {
  Bar,
  BarChart,
  CartesianGrid,
  Cell,
  Pie,
  PieChart,
  ResponsiveContainer,
  Tooltip,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
} from "recharts";
import { api } from "./api/client";
import "./styles.css";

const tabs = [
  { id: "dashboard", label: "Dashboard", icon: BarChart3 },
  { id: "gallery", label: "Galeria", icon: Images },
  { id: "map", label: "Mapa", icon: MapIcon },
  { id: "animals", label: "Animales", icon: Search },
  { id: "import", label: "Importar", icon: FileUp },
];

const palette = ["#1677ff", "#13a37f", "#d64550", "#e3a008", "#7c3aed", "#0891b2", "#64748b"];

function formatDate(value) {
  if (!value) return "";
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-ES").format(new Date(`${value}T00:00:00`));
}

function years(value) {
  if (!value) return "";
  const born = new Date(`${value}T00:00:00`);
  const diff = (Date.now() - born.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24 * 365.25);
  return `${diff.toFixed(1)} anos`;
}

function toQueryString(values) {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  Object.entries(values).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
      value.filter(Boolean).forEach((item) => params.append(key, item));
    } else if (value) {
      params.append(key, value);
    }
  });
  const suffix = params.toString();
  return suffix ? `?${suffix}` : "";
}

function AuthenticatedApp() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState({ loading: true, username: null });

  useEffect(() => {
    api.session()
      .then((result) => setSession({ loading: false, ...(result.authenticated ? result : { username: null }) }))
      .catch((error) => setSession({ loading: false, username: null, error: error.message }));

    function requireLogin() {
      setSession({ loading: false, username: null });
    }
    window.addEventListener("cowsia:auth-required", requireLogin);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("cowsia:auth-required", requireLogin);
  }, []);

  if (session.loading) {
    return <div className="auth-loading">Comprobando sesion...</div>;
  }
  if (!session.username) {
    return <Login initialError={session.error} onLogin={(result) => setSession({ loading: false, ...result })} />;
  }
  return (
    <App
      username={session.username}
      farmName={session.farm_name}
      canManageUsers={session.can_manage_users}
      onLogout={async () => {
        try {
          await api.logout();
        } finally {
          setSession({ loading: false, username: null });
        }
      }}
    />
  );
}

function Login({ initialError, onLogin }) {
  const [username, setUsername] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState(initialError || "");
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  async function submit(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setSubmitting(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const result = await api.login(username, password);
      onLogin(result);
    } catch (loginError) {
      setError(loginError.message);
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="login-page">
      <form className="login-card" onSubmit={submit}>
        <div className="login-brand"><LockKeyhole size={28} /></div>
        <h1>Acceso a CowsIA</h1>
        <p className="muted">Introduce tus credenciales para acceder al registro ganadero.</p>
        <label>
          Usuario
          <input
            autoComplete="username"
            autoFocus
            value={username}
            onChange={(event) => setUsername(event.target.value)}
            required
          />
        </label>
        <label>
          Contrasena
          <input
            type="password"
            autoComplete="current-password"
            value={password}
            onChange={(event) => setPassword(event.target.value)}
            required
          />
        </label>
        {error && <p className="error login-error">{error}</p>}
        <button className="primary" type="submit" disabled={submitting}>
          {submitting ? "Accediendo..." : "Entrar"}
        </button>
      </form>
    </main>
  );
}

function App({ username, farmName, canManageUsers, onLogout }) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState("dashboard");
  const [refreshKey, setRefreshKey] = useState(0);
  const visibleTabs = canManageUsers ? [...tabs, { id: "users", label: "Usuarios", icon: UserPlus }] : tabs;

  function refresh() {
    setRefreshKey((value) => value + 1);
  }

  return (
    <div className="app-shell">
      <aside className="sidebar">
        <div className="brand">
          <Gauge size={24} />
          <div>
            <strong>CowsIA</strong>
            <span>Registro ganadero</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <nav>
          {visibleTabs.map((tab) => {
            const Icon = tab.icon;
            return (
              <button key={tab.id} className={activeTab === tab.id ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)}>
                <Icon size={18} />
                {tab.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </nav>
        <div className="session-panel">
          <span title={username}>{username}</span>
          <small title={farmName}>{farmName}</small>
          <button type="button" onClick={onLogout}><LogOut size={16} />Salir</button>
        </div>
      </aside>
      <main>
        {activeTab === "dashboard" && <Dashboard refreshKey={refreshKey} />}
        {activeTab === "gallery" && <Gallery refreshKey={refreshKey} />}
        {activeTab === "map" && <CowsMap refreshKey={refreshKey} />}
        {activeTab === "animals" && <Animals refreshKey={refreshKey} />}
        {activeTab === "import" && <ImportXml onRefresh={refresh} />}
        {activeTab === "users" && canManageUsers && <Users />}
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

function Users() {
  const [users, setUsers] = useState([]);
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ username: "", password: "", farm_name: "" });
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  async function loadUsers() {
    setUsers(await api.get("/api/admin/users"));
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    loadUsers().catch((loadError) => setError(loadError.message));
  }, []);

  async function submit(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setMessage("");
    setError("");
    try {
      const created = await api.post("/api/admin/users", form);
      setMessage(`Usuario ${created.username} creado en ${created.farm_name}.`);
      setForm({ username: "", password: "", farm_name: "" });
      await loadUsers();
    } catch (createError) {
      setError(createError.message);
    }
  }

  return (
    <section className="page">
      <PageTitle title="Usuarios" />
      <div className="grid two users-grid">
        <form className="panel user-form" onSubmit={submit}>
          <h2>Crear usuario</h2>
          <label>
            Usuario
            <input value={form.username} onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, username: event.target.value })} required minLength={3} />
          </label>
          <label>
            Contrasena inicial
            <input type="password" value={form.password} onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, password: event.target.value })} required minLength={10} />
          </label>
          <label>
            Explotacion
            <input value={form.farm_name} onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, farm_name: event.target.value })} placeholder="Una existente o un nombre nuevo" required />
          </label>
          <p className="muted">Usa el mismo nombre de explotacion para que varios usuarios compartan sus animales.</p>
          <button className="primary" type="submit"><UserPlus size={16} />Crear usuario</button>
          {message && <p className="positive">{message}</p>}
          {error && <p className="error">{error}</p>}
        </form>
        <div className="panel">
          <h2>Usuarios existentes</h2>
          <div className="user-list">
            {users.map((user) => (
              <div className="user-row" key={user.id}>
                <strong>{user.username}</strong>
                <span>{user.farm_name}</span>
                <small>{user.active ? "Activo" : "Inactivo"}{user.can_manage_users ? " · Administrador" : ""}</small>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

function Dashboard({ refreshKey }) {
  const [data, setData] = useState({
    summary: {},
    status: [],
    sex: [],
    breeds: [],
    birthYears: [],
    exits: [],
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    Promise.all([
      api.get("/api/dashboard/summary"),
      api.get("/api/dashboard/status"),
      api.get("/api/dashboard/sex"),
      api.get("/api/dashboard/breeds"),
      api.get("/api/dashboard/birth-years"),
      api.get("/api/dashboard/exits"),
    ]).then(([summary, status, sex, breeds, birthYears, exits]) => {
      setData({ summary, status, sex, breeds, birthYears, exits });
    });
  }, [refreshKey]);

  return (
    <section className="page">
      <PageTitle title="Dashboard" action={<ExportButtons />} />
      <div className="metrics">
        <Metric label="Animales" value={data.summary.total || 0} />
        <Metric label="Activos" value={data.summary.active || 0} tone="good" />
        <Metric label="Bajas" value={data.summary.inactive || 0} tone="bad" />
        <Metric label="Edad media activos" value={`${data.summary.average_age || 0} anos`} />
      </div>
      <div className="grid two">
        <ChartPanel title="Nacimientos por ano">
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={280}>
            <BarChart data={data.birthYears}>
              <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" />
              <XAxis dataKey="year" />
              <YAxis allowDecimals={false} />
              <Tooltip />
              <Bar dataKey="animals" fill="#1677ff" name="Animales" />
            </BarChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
        </ChartPanel>
        <ChartPanel title="Bajas por causa">
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={280}>
            <BarChart data={data.exits} layout="vertical" margin={{ left: 24, right: 10 }}>
              <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" />
              <XAxis type="number" allowDecimals={false} />
              <YAxis type="category" dataKey="cause" width={120} />
              <Tooltip />
              <Bar dataKey="animals" fill="#d64550" name="Animales" />
            </BarChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
        </ChartPanel>
      </div>
      <div className="grid three">
        <PiePanel title="Estado" rows={data.status} />
        <PiePanel title="Sexo" rows={data.sex} />
        <ListPanel title="Razas" rows={data.breeds} />
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function Animals({ refreshKey }) {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState([]);
  const [selectedAnimal, setSelectedAnimal] = useState(null);
  const [options, setOptions] = useState({ sexos: [], razas: [], causas_baja: [], explotaciones: [] });
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState({
    q: "",
    status: "",
    sexo: "",
    raza: "",
    causa_baja: "",
    explotacion: "",
    born_from: "",
    born_to: "",
  });

  const query = useMemo(
    () =>
      toQueryString({
        ...filters,
        sexo: filters.sexo ? [filters.sexo] : [],
      }),
    [filters],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    api.get("/api/filter-options").then(setOptions);
  }, [refreshKey]);

  useEffect(() => {
    api.get(`/api/animals${query}`).then(setRows);
  }, [query, refreshKey]);

  function setFilter(name, value) {
    setFilters((current) => ({ ...current, [name]: value }));
  }

  return (
    <section className="page">
      <PageTitle title="Animales" action={<ExportButtons />} />
      <div className="toolbar">
        <label>
          <Search size={16} />
          <input placeholder="Crotal, madre, procedencia o destino" value={filters.q} onChange={(event) => setFilter("q", event.target.value)} />
        </label>
        <select value={filters.status} onChange={(event) => setFilter("status", event.target.value)}>
          <option value="">Todos los estados</option>
          <option value="Activo">Activos</option>
          <option value="Baja">Bajas</option>
        </select>
        <select value={filters.sexo} onChange={(event) => setFilter("sexo", event.target.value)}>
          <option value="">Todos los sexos</option>
          {options.sexos.map((item) => (
            <option key={item} value={item}>{item}</option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <select value={filters.raza} onChange={(event) => setFilter("raza", event.target.value)}>
          <option value="">Todas las razas</option>
          {options.razas.map((item) => (
            <option key={item} value={item}>{item}</option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <select value={filters.causa_baja} onChange={(event) => setFilter("causa_baja", event.target.value)}>
          <option value="">Todas las causas</option>
          {options.causas_baja.map((item) => (
            <option key={item} value={item}>{item}</option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <select value={filters.explotacion} onChange={(event) => setFilter("explotacion", event.target.value)}>
          <option value="">Todas las explotaciones</option>
          {options.explotaciones.map((item) => (
            <option key={item} value={item}>{item}</option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <input type="date" value={filters.born_from} onChange={(event) => setFilter("born_from", event.target.value)} />
        <input type="date" value={filters.born_to} onChange={(event) => setFilter("born_to", event.target.value)} />
      </div>
      <div className="result-strip">
        <ClipboardList size={18} />
        <strong>{rows.length}</strong>
        <span>registros</span>
      </div>
      <div className="table-wrap">
        <table>
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Crotal</th>
              <th>Estado</th>
              <th>Nacimiento</th>
              <th>Edad</th>
              <th>Sexo</th>
              <th>Raza</th>
              <th>Madre</th>
              <th>Alta</th>
              <th>Entrada</th>
              <th>Baja</th>
              <th>Destino</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {rows.map((row) => (
              <tr key={row.id}>
                <td>
                  <button className="link-button" onClick={() => setSelectedAnimal(row)}>
                    <strong>{row.crotal}</strong>
                  </button>
                </td>
                <td><span className={`pill ${row.fecha_baja || row.causa_baja ? "bad" : "good"}`}>{row.fecha_baja || row.causa_baja ? "Baja" : "Activo"}</span></td>
                <td>{formatDate(row.fecha_nacimiento)}</td>
                <td>{years(row.fecha_nacimiento)}</td>
                <td>{row.sexo || ""}</td>
                <td>{row.raza || ""}</td>
                <td>{row.crotal_madre || ""}</td>
                <td>{row.causa_alta || ""} {formatDate(row.fecha_alta)}</td>
                <td>{row.procedencia || row.codigo_local_entrada || ""}</td>
                <td>{row.causa_baja || ""} {formatDate(row.fecha_baja)}</td>
                <td>{row.destino || row.codigo_local_salida || ""}</td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
      {selectedAnimal && <AnimalDetail animal={selectedAnimal} onClose={() => setSelectedAnimal(null)} />}
    </section>
  );
}

function PiePanel({ title, rows }) {
  return (
    <ChartPanel title={title}>
      <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={250}>
        <PieChart>
          <Pie data={rows} dataKey="value" nameKey="name" outerRadius={86}>
            {rows.map((_, index) => (
              <Cell key={index} fill={palette[index % palette.length]} />
            ))}
          </Pie>
          <Tooltip />
        </PieChart>
      </ResponsiveContainer>
    </ChartPanel>
  );
}

function ListPanel({ title, rows }) {
  return (
    <div className="panel list-panel">
      <h2>{title}</h2>
      {rows.length === 0 && <p className="muted">Sin datos</p>}
      {rows.map((row) => (
        <div className="list-row" key={row.name}>
          <span>{row.name}</span>
          <strong>{row.value}</strong>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

function PageTitle({ title, action }) {
  return (
    <header className="page-title">
      <h1>{title}</h1>
      {action}
    </header>
  );
}

function Metric({ label, value, tone }) {
  return (
    <div className={`metric ${tone || ""}`}>
      <span>{label}</span>
      <strong>{value}</strong>
    </div>
  );
}

function ChartPanel({ title, children }) {
  return (
    <div className="panel">
      <h2>{title}</h2>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from datetime import date

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.models import Animal


def age_years(born: date | None, today: date | None = None) -> float | None:
    if not born:
        return None
    today = today or date.today()
    return round((today - born).days / 365.25, 1)


def status(animal: Animal) -> str:
    return "Baja" if animal.fecha_baja or animal.causa_baja else "Activo"


def _animals(db: Session, farm_id: int) -> list[Animal]:
    return db.query(Animal).filter(Animal.farm_id == farm_id).all()


def summary(db: Session, farm_id: int) -> dict:
    animals = _animals(db, farm_id)
    active = [animal for animal in animals if status(animal) == "Activo"]
    baja = len(animals) - len(active)
    females = sum(1 for animal in animals if (animal.sexo or "").casefold() == "hembra")
    males = sum(1 for animal in animals if (animal.sexo or "").casefold() == "macho")
    ages = [age_years(animal.fecha_nacimiento) for animal in active]
    ages = [value for value in ages if value is not None]
    return {
        "total": len(animals),
        "active": len(active),
        "inactive": baja,
        "females": females,
        "males": males,
        "average_age": round(sum(ages) / len(ages), 1) if ages else 0,
    }


def by_status(db: Session, farm_id: int) -> list[dict]:
    counts = Counter(status(animal) for animal in _animals(db, farm_id))
    return [{"name": key, "value": value} for key, value in counts.items()]


def by_sex(db: Session, farm_id: int) -> list[dict]:
    counts = Counter(animal.sexo or "Sin dato" for animal in _animals(db, farm_id))
    return [{"name": key, "value": value} for key, value in counts.items()]


def by_breed(db: Session, farm_id: int) -> list[dict]:
    counts = Counter(animal.raza or "Sin dato" for animal in _animals(db, farm_id))
    return [{"name": key, "value": value} for key, value in counts.most_common()]


def by_birth_year(db: Session, farm_id: int) -> list[dict]:
    counts: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    for animal in _animals(db, farm_id):
        key = str(animal.fecha_nacimiento.year) if animal.fecha_nacimiento else "Sin fecha"
        counts[key] += 1
    return [{"year": key, "animals": counts[key]} for key in sorted(counts)]


def exits_by_cause(db: Session, farm_id: int) -> list[dict]:
    counts = Counter(animal.causa_baja or "Activo" for animal in _animals(db, farm_id))
    return [{"cause": key, "animals": value} for key, value in counts.most_common()]

  <script>
    const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('.feature-menu button');
    const panels = document.querySelectorAll('.feature-panel');
    tabs.forEach((tab) => tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
      tabs.forEach((item) => { item.classList.remove('active'); item.setAttribute('aria-selected', 'false'); });
      panels.forEach((panel) => panel.classList.remove('active'));
      tab.classList.add('active');
      tab.setAttribute('aria-selected', 'true');
      document.getElementById(`panel-${tab.dataset.panel}`).classList.add('active');
    }));

    const menuButton = document.querySelector('.menu-toggle');
    const nav = document.querySelector('.nav');
    menuButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const open = nav.classList.toggle('open');
      menuButton.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(open));
    });
    nav.querySelectorAll('a').forEach((link) => link.addEventListener('click', () => {
      nav.classList.remove('open');
      menuButton.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    }));

    const sectionLinks = [...nav.querySelectorAll('a')];
    const observed = sectionLinks.map((link) => document.querySelector(link.getAttribute('href'))).filter(Boolean);
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
      entries.forEach((entry) => {
        if (entry.isIntersecting) {
          sectionLinks.forEach((link) => link.classList.toggle('active', link.getAttribute('href') === `#${entry.target.id}`));
        }
      });
    }, { rootMargin: '-25% 0px -65%' });
    observed.forEach((section) => observer.observe(section));
    document.getElementById('year').textContent = new Date().getFullYear();
  </script>

# OpenAI Docs

Provide current, cited OpenAI product, API, model, and Codex guidance. Read zero or one primary reference.

**First substantive action:** Search the user's exact requested official OpenAI documentation topic and any explicitly named model using a concise, topic-specific query of 2-6 essential terms. When an already-available direct official documentation search and page-retrieval capability is present, use it first: search, then fetch or open the matching official page before general web search. Otherwise, immediately use official-domain web search, then actually open or fetch the relevant official page. Complete this source order before reading a reference, inspecting local or repository files, running a Codex manual or model resolver, drafting a plan, or answering from memory. Use the actual fetched page, not a search snippet or an unopened link. If one official search or page does not establish the answer, search another appropriate official domain and actually open or fetch the result. Preserve the exact requested model; never substitute a newer model.

**Only exception:** An explicitly requested, genuinely broad, cross-topic Codex setup, orientation, or system-map synthesis may use the manual first when shell execution and an allowed temporary cache are available. A specific Codex feature, setting, command, error, model, or requested citation remains docs-first. Mixed Chat/Work/Codex comparisons are official documentation questions, not manual-first Codex requests.

For generic software tasks, answer the software task directly. OpenAI implementation, debugging, SDK, API, prompting, agent, and eval requests are not generic.

For a straightforward factual or citation-only request, follow the source order and do not read a route reference. This includes straightforward API facts, ChatGPT Work or mixed Chat/Work/Codex comparisons, model tiers, aliases, Pro mode, reasoning settings, factual migration baselines, and narrow Codex facts. Prioritize `learn.chatgpt.com` for ChatGPT Work.

## Choose one primary route

Use the first matching route, and read its reference only when the requested task needs that specialized workflow:

- **Explicitly requested local documentation integration:** Read [integration guidance](references/mcp-diagnostics.md) only when the user explicitly requests that local integration.
- **Model migration, upgrades, or model-specific prompting:** Read [model-migration.md](references/model-migration.md) for actual migration planning, implementation, dynamic target resolution, or prompt changes. Preserve an explicitly requested target.
- **Model selection and comparisons:** Read [model-selection.md](references/model-selection.md) only when nuanced current, latest, default, cost, latency, quality, or modality tradeoffs need more guidance. Do not run a migration resolver for selection alone.
- **Product, API, ChatGPT Work, and mixed Chat/Work/Codex documentation:** Read [official-docs.md](references/official-docs.md) only when fetched official pages leave source selection, API schemas, or the requested implementation unresolved. This route is not manual-first.
- **Explicitly broad Codex setup, orientation, or cross-topic synthesis:** Read [codex-self-knowledge.md](references/codex-self-knowledge.md) when the eligible Codex manual or deeper Codex procedures are needed.

Read at most one primary reference. Do not open every route, bundled model guide, or helper script. Read a supporting reference or run a helper only when the chosen workflow demonstrably needs it.

## Source and execution boundaries

- Search, open, fetch, and cite only `developers.openai.com`, `platform.openai.com`, and `learn.chatgpt.com`. Cite the page that supports the claim. State uncertainty when official sources do not establish pricing, availability, account access, limits, or behavior.

- Before building, running, editing, debugging, or testing an API-backed app or tool, use `openai-platform-api-key` first when available. Documentation, conceptual examples, model selection, and read-only guidance do not require an API key.

Assistant

Sí, se puede solventar. En este caso había dos bloqueos distintos, no una incompatibilidad de CowsIA:

  1. CowsIA no estaba escuchando en localhost:8081.
  2. Docker y Firefox estaban instalados, pero el sandbox impedía acceder al socket de Docker y Firefox era una instalación Snap incompatible con ese aislamiento.

1. Arrancar CowsIA

Desde el servidor anfitrión:

cd /opt/DesplieguesDocker/CowsIA
docker compose up --build -d
docker compose ps
curl -I http://127.0.0.1:8081/

Si Docker responde con permission denied ... /var/run/docker.sock, puedes:

sudo docker compose up --build -d

O configurar permanentemente el usuario:

sudo usermod -aG docker "$USER"

Después habría que cerrar sesión y volver a entrar. Ten en cuenta que pertenecer al grupo docker concede privilegios muy elevados.

En una sesión de Codex también puedo solicitar una aprobación específica para ejecutar docker compose, sin necesidad de desactivar todo el aislamiento. El modo recomendado es workspace-write con aprobaciones on-request; Codex pregunta cuando necesita superar el límite del sandbox. Documentación oficial sobre sandbox y aprobaciones.

2. Utilizar un navegador compatible

El Firefox encontrado es una instalación Snap y falla porque snap-confine no recibe las capacidades necesarias. La solución más práctica para capturas automatizadas es Playwright con su propio Chromium:

cd /opt/DesplieguesDocker/CowsIA/frontend
npm install --save-dev playwright
npx playwright install chromium

Si faltan bibliotecas del sistema:

sudo npx playwright install-deps chromium

Esto evita depender de Firefox Snap.

3. Capturar la aplicación autenticada

Se puede crear un script frontend/scripts/capturas.mjs que:

  • Abra http://127.0.0.1:8081.
  • Obtenga usuario y contraseña desde variables de entorno.
  • Inicie sesión sin mostrar las credenciales.
  • Visite Dashboard, Galería, Mapa y Animales.
  • Genere PNG completos.
  • Actualice cowsia.html para utilizar esas capturas reales.

Una ejecución segura tendría esta forma:

set -a
source /opt/DesplieguesDocker/CowsIA/.env
set +a

node frontend/scripts/capturas.mjs

Las imágenes podrían guardarse en:

capturas/dashboard.png
capturas/galeria.png
capturas/mapa.png
capturas/animales.png

Conviene revisar antes las capturas porque pueden mostrar crotales, nombres de explotación, ubicaciones u otros datos reales.

4. Configuración recomendada para Codex

No necesitas darle acceso completo. Basta con iniciar Codex con aprobaciones bajo demanda:

codex --sandbox workspace-write --ask-for-approval on-request

Así podré pedirte autorización únicamente para:

  • Acceder al socket de Docker.
  • Descargar Chromium si todavía no está instalado.
  • Ejecutar el navegador fuera del aislamiento cuando sea necesario.

El modo danger-full-access eliminaría las restricciones, pero la documentación oficial recomienda usarlo con cautela; para este trabajo no debería ser necesario. Descripción oficial de los modos de sandbox.

Si quieres, puedo realizar ahora el proceso completo solicitando las aprobaciones necesarias y reemplazar la recreación de cowsia.html por capturas reales.

User

Si, hazlo

Assistant

Voy a arrancar CowsIA mediante Docker, verificar el acceso en 8081, instalar un Chromium automatizable si hace falta y generar capturas autenticadas sin imprimir las credenciales. Después sustituiré la recreación del HTML por imágenes reales, revisándolas antes para evitar información innecesariamente sensible.

User

codex --sandbox workspace-write --ask-for-approval on-request

Assistant

Ese comando debe ejecutarse en tu terminal para iniciar una sesión nueva de Codex; no conviene lanzarlo dentro de esta sesión porque abriría otro agente interactivo. Esta sesión ya está en workspace-write con aprobaciones administradas. La solicitud anterior se canceló, así que vuelvo a pedir únicamente el permiso necesario para docker compose; acepta el diálogo para que pueda continuar.

$ sed -n '293,399p' frontend/src/main.jsx; sed -n '1,120p' frontend/package.json; rg -n 'gallery|map-shell|map-container|table-wrap|leaflet' frontend/src/styles.css | sed -n '1,120p'
function CowsMap({ refreshKey }) {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState([]);
  const [selectedAnimal, setSelectedAnimal] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    api.get("/api/map/locations").then(setRows);
  }, [refreshKey]);

  const center = useMemo(() => {
    if (rows.length === 0) return [39.96, -6.48];
    const lat = rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.latitude, 0) / rows.length;
    const lng = rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.longitude, 0) / rows.length;
    return [lat, lng];
  }, [rows]);

  return (
    <section className="page map-page">
      <PageTitle title="Mapa" />
      <div className="map-summary">
        <span>{rows.length} animales con ubicacion</span>
      </div>
      <div className="map-panel">
        <MapContainer key={`${center[0]}-${center[1]}-${rows.length}`} center={center} zoom={15} scrollWheelZoom className="animal-map">
          <TileLayer
            attribution='&copy; <a href="https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright">OpenStreetMap</a>'
            url="https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png"
          />
          {rows.map((row) => (
            <Marker key={`${row.animal_id}-${row.latitude}-${row.longitude}`} position={[row.latitude, row.longitude]} icon={animalMarkerIcon}>
              <Popup>
                <div className="map-popup">
                  {row.image_url && <img src={api.url(row.image_url)} alt={row.crotal} />}
                  <strong>{row.crotal}</strong>
                  <span>{row.sexo || "Sin sexo"} · {row.age_years === null ? "Sin edad" : `${row.age_years} anos`}</span>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() =>
                      setSelectedAnimal({
                        crotal: row.crotal,
                        sexo: row.sexo,
                        fecha_nacimiento: row.fecha_nacimiento,
                      })
                    }
                  >
                    Abrir ficha
                  </button>
                </div>
              </Popup>
            </Marker>
          ))}
        </MapContainer>
      </div>
      {selectedAnimal && <AnimalDetail animal={selectedAnimal} onClose={() => setSelectedAnimal(null)} onChanged={() => api.get("/api/map/locations").then(setRows)} />}
    </section>
  );
}

function Gallery({ refreshKey }) {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState([]);
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [selectedAnimal, setSelectedAnimal] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    api.get("/api/gallery").then(setRows);
  }, [refreshKey]);

  const filteredRows = useMemo(() => {
    const term = q.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!term) return rows;
    return rows.filter((row) => row.crotal.toLowerCase().includes(term) || (row.sexo || "").toLowerCase().includes(term));
  }, [rows, q]);

  return (
    <section className="page">
      <PageTitle title="Galeria" />
      <div className="gallery-toolbar">
        <label>
          <Search size={16} />
          <input placeholder="Buscar crotal o sexo" value={q} onChange={(event) => setQ(event.target.value)} />
        </label>
        <span>{filteredRows.length} animales</span>
      </div>
      <div className="gallery-grid">
        {filteredRows.map((row) => (
          <button
            className="gallery-card"
            key={row.id}
            onClick={() => setSelectedAnimal({ crotal: row.crotal, sexo: row.sexo, fecha_nacimiento: row.fecha_nacimiento })}
          >
            {row.image_url ? (
              <img src={api.url(row.image_url)} alt={row.crotal} />
            ) : (
              <div className="gallery-placeholder"><Image size={28} /></div>
            )}
            <div>
              <strong>{row.crotal}</strong>
              <span>{row.sexo || "Sin sexo"} · {row.age_years === null ? "Sin edad" : `${row.age_years} anos`}</span>
            </div>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      {selectedAnimal && <AnimalDetail animal={selectedAnimal} onClose={() => setSelectedAnimal(null)} onChanged={() => api.get("/api/gallery").then(setRows)} />}
    </section>
  );
}

import { mkdir } from "node:fs/promises";
import { resolve } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { chromium } from "playwright";

const scriptDirectory = fileURLToPath(new URL(".", import.meta.url));
const projectRoot = resolve(scriptDirectory, "../..");
const outputDirectory = resolve(projectRoot, "capturas_cowsia");
const baseUrl = process.env.COWSIA_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:8081";
const username = process.env.COWSIA_DEMO_USERNAME || process.env.COWSIA_USERNAME;
const password = process.env.COWSIA_DEMO_PASSWORD || process.env.COWSIA_PASSWORD;

if (!username || !password) {
  throw new Error("Faltan COWSIA_DEMO_USERNAME/COWSIA_DEMO_PASSWORD o las credenciales principales.");
}

await mkdir(outputDirectory, { recursive: true });

const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true });
const context = await browser.newContext({
  viewport: { width: 1440, height: 960 },
  deviceScaleFactor: 1,
  colorScheme: "light",
  locale: "es-ES",
});
const page = await context.newPage();

async function settle() {
  await page.waitForTimeout(1200);
  await page.evaluate(() => {
    document.querySelectorAll("*").forEach((element) => {
      element.style.animationDuration = "0s";
      element.style.transitionDuration = "0s";
    });
  });
}

async function capture(name) {
  await settle();
  await page.screenshot({
    path: resolve(outputDirectory, `${name}.png`),
    fullPage: false,
  });
}

try {
  await page.goto(baseUrl, { waitUntil: "domcontentloaded" });

  if (await page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Acceso a CowsIA" }).isVisible().catch(() => false)) {
    await page.getByLabel("Usuario").fill(username);
    await page.getByLabel("Contrasena").fill(password);
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Entrar" }).click();
  }

  await page.locator(".metrics").waitFor({ state: "visible" });
  await capture("dashboard");

  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Galeria" }).click();
  await page.locator(".gallery-card").first().waitFor({ state: "visible" });
  await capture("galeria");

  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Animales" }).click();
  await page.locator(".table-wrap tbody tr").first().waitFor({ state: "visible" });
  await capture("animales");

  console.log(`Capturas guardadas en ${outputDirectory}`);
} finally {
  await browser.close();
}

$ sed -n '590,778p' frontend/src/main.jsx
function AnimalDetail({ animal, onClose, onChanged }) {
  const [detail, setDetail] = useState(null);
  const [imageFile, setImageFile] = useState(null);
  const [eventFile, setEventFile] = useState(null);
  const [locationForm, setLocationForm] = useState({ latitude: "", longitude: "", recorded_at: "", source: "" });
  const [eventForm, setEventForm] = useState({ text: "", event_date: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10), reporter: "" });
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  async function load() {
    setDetail(await api.get(`/api/animals/${encodeURIComponent(animal.crotal)}/details`));
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    load().catch((exc) => setError(exc.message));
  }, [animal.crotal]);

  async function uploadAnimalImage(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!imageFile) return;
    setError("");
    try {
      await api.upload(`/api/animals/${encodeURIComponent(animal.crotal)}/images`, imageFile);
      setImageFile(null);
      await load();
      onChanged?.();
    } catch (exc) {
      setError(exc.message);
    }
  }

  async function saveLocation(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    try {
      await api.post(`/api/animals/${encodeURIComponent(animal.crotal)}/locations`, {
        latitude: Number(locationForm.latitude),
        longitude: Number(locationForm.longitude),
        recorded_at: locationForm.recorded_at ? new Date(locationForm.recorded_at).toISOString() : null,
        source: locationForm.source || null,
      });
      setLocationForm({ latitude: "", longitude: "", recorded_at: "", source: "" });
      await load();
      onChanged?.();
    } catch (exc) {
      setError(exc.message);
    }
  }

  async function saveEvent(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    try {
      const form = new FormData();
      form.append("text", eventForm.text);
      if (eventForm.event_date) form.append("event_date", eventForm.event_date);
      if (eventForm.reporter) form.append("reporter", eventForm.reporter);
      if (eventFile) form.append("file", eventFile);
      await api.uploadForm(`/api/animals/${encodeURIComponent(animal.crotal)}/events-with-image`, form);
      setEventForm({ text: "", event_date: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10), reporter: "" });
      setEventFile(null);
      await load();
      onChanged?.();
    } catch (exc) {
      setError(exc.message);
    }
  }

  async function remove(path) {
    setError("");
    try {
      await api.delete(path);
      await load();
      onChanged?.();
    } catch (exc) {
      setError(exc.message);
    }
  }

  async function setPrimary(imageId) {
    setError("");
    try {
      await api.post(`/api/animal-images/${imageId}/primary`, {});
      await load();
      onChanged?.();
    } catch (exc) {
      setError(exc.message);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="detail-backdrop">
      <aside className="detail-panel">
        <header className="detail-header">
          <div>
            <span className="muted">Ficha del animal</span>
            <h2>{animal.crotal}</h2>
          </div>
          <button className="icon" onClick={onClose} title="Cerrar"><X size={18} /></button>
        </header>
        <div className="animal-summary">
          <span>{animal.sexo || "Sin sexo"}</span>
          <span>{animal.raza || "Sin raza"}</span>
          <span>{formatDate(animal.fecha_nacimiento) || "Sin nacimiento"}</span>
        </div>
        {error && <p className="error">{error}</p>}
        {!detail && <p className="muted">Cargando...</p>}
        {detail && (
          <div className="detail-content">
            <section className="detail-section">
              <h3><Image size={16} /> Imagenes</h3>
              <form className="compact-form" onSubmit={uploadAnimalImage}>
                <input type="file" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp,image/gif" onChange={(event) => setImageFile(event.target.files?.[0] || null)} />
                <button disabled={!imageFile}><Plus size={16} />Anadir</button>
              </form>
              <div className="image-grid">
                {detail.images.map((row) => (
                  <div className={`image-item ${row.is_primary ? "primary" : ""}`} key={row.id}>
                    <img src={api.url(row.image_url)} alt={row.file_name || "Imagen del animal"} />
                    <div>
                      <span>{row.file_name || "Imagen"}</span>
                      <div className="image-actions">
                        <button className="icon" onClick={() => setPrimary(row.id)} disabled={row.is_primary} title="Imagen principal">
                          <Image size={15} />
                        </button>
                        <button className="icon" onClick={() => remove(`/api/animal-images/${row.id}`)} title="Eliminar"><Trash2 size={15} /></button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    {row.is_primary && <b>Principal</b>}
                  </div>
                ))}
                {detail.images.length === 0 && <p className="muted">Sin imagenes</p>}
              </div>
            </section>
            <section className="detail-section">
              <h3><MapPin size={16} /> Ubicaciones</h3>
              {detail.locations.length > 0 && <AnimalLocationMiniMap locations={detail.locations} />}
              <form className="compact-form location-form" onSubmit={saveLocation}>
                <input required type="number" step="0.000001" placeholder="Latitud" value={locationForm.latitude} onChange={(event) => setLocationForm({ ...locationForm, latitude: event.target.value })} />
                <input required type="number" step="0.000001" placeholder="Longitud" value={locationForm.longitude} onChange={(event) => setLocationForm({ ...locationForm, longitude: event.target.value })} />
                <input type="datetime-local" value={locationForm.recorded_at} onChange={(event) => setLocationForm({ ...locationForm, recorded_at: event.target.value })} />
                <input placeholder="Origen" value={locationForm.source} onChange={(event) => setLocationForm({ ...locationForm, source: event.target.value })} />
                <button><Plus size={16} />Anadir</button>
              </form>
              <div className="stack-list">
                {detail.locations.map((row) => (
                  <div className="stack-row" key={row.id}>
                    <MapPin size={15} />
                    <span>{row.latitude}, {row.longitude}</span>
                    <a className="icon" href={`https://www.google.com/maps?q=${row.latitude},${row.longitude}`} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" title="Abrir mapa"><ExternalLink size={15} /></a>
                    <button className="icon" onClick={() => remove(`/api/animal-locations/${row.id}`)} title="Eliminar"><Trash2 size={15} /></button>
                  </div>
                ))}
                {detail.locations.length === 0 && <p className="muted">Sin ubicaciones</p>}
              </div>
            </section>
            <section className="detail-section">
              <h3><CalendarPlus size={16} /> Sucesos</h3>
              <form className="event-form" onSubmit={saveEvent}>
                <textarea required placeholder="Texto del suceso" value={eventForm.text} onChange={(event) => setEventForm({ ...eventForm, text: event.target.value })} />
                <div className="compact-form">
                  <input type="date" value={eventForm.event_date} onChange={(event) => setEventForm({ ...eventForm, event_date: event.target.value })} />
                  <input placeholder="Usuario informante" value={eventForm.reporter} onChange={(event) => setEventForm({ ...eventForm, reporter: event.target.value })} />
                  <input type="file" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp,image/gif" onChange={(event) => setEventFile(event.target.files?.[0] || null)} />
                  <button><Plus size={16} />Anadir</button>
                </div>
              </form>
              <div className="event-list">
                {detail.events.map((row) => (
                  <article className="event-row" key={row.id}>
                    <header>
                      <strong>{formatDate(row.event_date) || "Sin fecha"}</strong>
                      <span>{row.reporter || "Sin usuario"}</span>
                      <button className="icon" onClick={() => remove(`/api/animal-events/${row.id}`)} title="Eliminar"><Trash2 size={15} /></button>
                    </header>
                    <p>{row.text}</p>
                    {row.image_url && <img src={api.url(row.image_url)} alt={row.image_name || "Imagen del suceso"} />}
                  </article>
                ))}
                {detail.events.length === 0 && <p className="muted">Sin sucesos</p>}
              </div>
            </section>
          </div>
        )}
      </aside>
    </div>
  );
}

import { access, mkdir } from "node:fs/promises";
import { resolve } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { chromium } from "playwright";

const scriptDirectory = fileURLToPath(new URL(".", import.meta.url));
const projectRoot = resolve(scriptDirectory, "../..");
const outputDirectory = resolve(projectRoot, "capturas_cowsia");
const baseUrl = process.env.COWSIA_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:8081";
const username = process.env.COWSIA_DEMO_USERNAME || process.env.COWSIA_USERNAME;
const password = process.env.COWSIA_DEMO_PASSWORD || process.env.COWSIA_PASSWORD;
const crotal = process.env.COWSIA_EVENT_CROTAL || "ES031007338630";
const eventText = process.env.COWSIA_EVENT_TEXT || "le falta un crotal";
const eventImage = resolve(projectRoot, process.env.COWSIA_EVENT_IMAGE || "imagenes_animales/8630_2.JPG");

if (!username || !password) {
  throw new Error("Faltan las credenciales de CowsIA en el entorno.");
}
await access(eventImage);
await mkdir(outputDirectory, { recursive: true });

const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true });
const page = await browser.newPage({
  viewport: { width: 1440, height: 1000 },
  deviceScaleFactor: 1,
  colorScheme: "light",
  locale: "es-ES",
});

async function settle() {
  await page.waitForTimeout(1000);
  await page.evaluate(() => {
    document.querySelectorAll("*").forEach((element) => {
      element.style.animationDuration = "0s";
      element.style.transitionDuration = "0s";
    });
  });
}

try {
  await page.goto(baseUrl, { waitUntil: "domcontentloaded" });
  if (await page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Acceso a CowsIA" }).isVisible().catch(() => false)) {
    await page.getByLabel("Usuario").fill(username);
    await page.getByLabel("Contrasena").fill(password);
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Entrar" }).click();
  }

  await page.locator(".metrics").waitFor({ state: "visible" });
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Animales" }).click();
  const search = page.getByPlaceholder("Crotal, madre, procedencia o destino");
  await search.fill(crotal);
  const animalLink = page.getByRole("button", { name: crotal, exact: true });
  await animalLink.waitFor({ state: "visible" });
  await animalLink.click();

  const detailPanel = page.locator(".detail-panel");
  await detailPanel.waitFor({ state: "visible" });
  await page.locator(".detail-content").waitFor({ state: "visible" });

  const eventsSection = page.locator(".detail-section").filter({ has: page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Sucesos" }) });
  const matchingEvent = eventsSection.locator(".event-row").filter({ hasText: eventText });
  const alreadyComplete = await matchingEvent.locator("img").count() > 0;

  if (!alreadyComplete) {
    await eventsSection.getByPlaceholder("Texto del suceso").fill(eventText);
    await eventsSection.locator('input[type="file"]').setInputFiles(eventImage);
    await eventsSection.getByRole("button", { name: "Anadir" }).click();
    await eventsSection.locator(".event-row").filter({ hasText: eventText }).locator("img").first().waitFor({ state: "visible" });
  }

  await detailPanel.evaluate((element) => { element.scrollTop = 0; });
  await settle();
  await page.screenshot({ path: resolve(outputDirectory, "ficha-animal.png"), fullPage: false });

  await eventsSection.scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
  await settle();
  await page.screenshot({ path: resolve(outputDirectory, "ficha-sucesos.png"), fullPage: false });

  console.log(`${alreadyComplete ? "Suceso existente" : "Suceso creado"}: ${eventText}`);
  console.log(`Animal: ${crotal}`);
} finally {
  await browser.close();
}

  <script>
    const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('.feature-menu button');
    const panels = document.querySelectorAll('.feature-panel');
    tabs.forEach((tab) => tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
      tabs.forEach((item) => { item.classList.remove('active'); item.setAttribute('aria-selected', 'false'); });
      panels.forEach((panel) => panel.classList.remove('active'));
      tab.classList.add('active');
      tab.setAttribute('aria-selected', 'true');
      document.getElementById(`panel-${tab.dataset.panel}`).classList.add('active');
    }));

    const menuButton = document.querySelector('.menu-toggle');
    const nav = document.querySelector('.nav');
    menuButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const open = nav.classList.toggle('open');
      menuButton.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(open));
    });
    nav.querySelectorAll('a').forEach((link) => link.addEventListener('click', () => {
      nav.classList.remove('open');
      menuButton.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    }));

    const sectionLinks = [...nav.querySelectorAll('a')];
    const observed = sectionLinks.map((link) => document.querySelector(link.getAttribute('href'))).filter(Boolean);
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
      entries.forEach((entry) => {
        if (entry.isIntersecting) {
          sectionLinks.forEach((link) => link.classList.toggle('active', link.getAttribute('href') === `#${entry.target.id}`));
        }
      });
    }, { rootMargin: '-25% 0px -65%' });
    observed.forEach((section) => observer.observe(section));
    document.getElementById('year').textContent = new Date().getFullYear();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

User

Podrias incrementar en el html el apartado de "Ficha individual y procesos" indicando que dicha funcionalidad al hacerse desde un terminal movil coge la fecha del suceso, la ubicación y el usuario logado para tener un historico de sucesos y que los sucesos pueden ser incidentes sobre animales (herida, falta de un crotal deteccion de comportamiento anomalo, ubicación fuera de la explotación, fallecimiento, ... y sucesos a animales que puedas sugerirme) así com oque puede usarse esta funcionalidad sin asociar ningun animal para anotar algun incidente en la explotación (alambrada rota, deteccion de fauna silvestre por huellas,... y situaciones que puedan darse en una finca rustica en un sitio determinado)

Assistant

Voy a ampliar esa sección con dos niveles claramente diferenciados: el funcionamiento que ya muestra la captura y una propuesta de uso móvil. La distinción es importante porque la versión actual asigna la fecha por defecto, pero todavía pide el informante manualmente, no captura GPS dentro del suceso y exige asociarlo a un animal. El HTML presentará fecha/hora, ubicación, usuario automático e incidencias generales de finca como evolución funcional, evitando afirmar que ya están implementadas.

class Farm(Base):
    __tablename__ = "farms"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), unique=True, index=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)


class User(Base):
    __tablename__ = "users"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    username: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(80), unique=True, index=True)
    password_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(300))
    farm_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("farms.id"), index=True)

    active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True, index=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)

from collections.abc import Generator
import os

from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy import inspect, text
from sqlalchemy.orm import DeclarativeBase, Session, sessionmaker

DATABASE_URL = "sqlite:///./cows.db"

engine = create_engine(DATABASE_URL, connect_args={"check_same_thread": False})
SessionLocal = sessionmaker(bind=engine, autoflush=False, autocommit=False)


class Base(DeclarativeBase):
    pass


def get_db() -> Generator[Session, None, None]:
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        yield db
    finally:
        db.close()


def init_db() -> None:
    from app import models  # noqa: F401

    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
    ensure_bootstrap_users()
    ensure_sqlite_columns()
    ensure_primary_animal_images()


def ensure_bootstrap_users() -> None:
    from app.auth import hash_password
    from app.models import Farm, User

    with SessionLocal() as db:
        if db.query(User).count():
            return
        username = os.getenv("COWSIA_USERNAME", "").strip()
        password = os.getenv("COWSIA_PASSWORD", "")
        farm_name = os.getenv("COWSIA_FARM_NAME", "explotacion_pepe").strip()
        if not username or not password or not farm_name:
            return
        farm = Farm(name=farm_name)
        db.add(farm)
        db.flush()
        db.add(
            User(
                username=username,
                password_hash=hash_password(password),
                farm_id=farm.id,
                can_manage_users=True,
                active=True,
            )
        )
        db.commit()


def ensure_sqlite_columns() -> None:
    inspector = inspect(engine)
    tables = inspector.get_table_names()
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        if "animal_images" in tables:
            columns = {column["name"] for column in inspector.get_columns("animal_images")}
            if "is_primary" not in columns:
                connection.execute(text("ALTER TABLE animal_images ADD COLUMN is_primary BOOLEAN DEFAULT 0"))


def ensure_primary_animal_images() -> None:
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        tables = inspect(engine).get_table_names()
        if "animal_images" not in tables:
            return
        animal_ids = [
            row[0]
            for row in connection.execute(text("SELECT DISTINCT animal_id FROM animal_images")).fetchall()
        ]
        for animal_id in animal_ids:
            primary_count = connection.execute(
                text("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM animal_images WHERE animal_id = :animal_id AND is_primary = 1"),
                {"animal_id": animal_id},
            ).scalar_one()
            if primary_count:
                continue
            first_image_id = connection.execute(
                text("SELECT id FROM animal_images WHERE animal_id = :animal_id ORDER BY created_at ASC, id ASC LIMIT 1"),
                {"animal_id": animal_id},
            ).scalar_one_or_none()
            if first_image_id is not None:
                connection.execute(
                    text("UPDATE animal_images SET is_primary = 1 WHERE id = :image_id"),
                    {"image_id": first_image_id},
                )

    password_is_valid,
    request_user_id,

from app.models import Animal, AnimalEvent, AnimalImage, AnimalLocation, Farm, User
from app.schemas import AnimalDetailOut, AnimalEventIn, AnimalEventOut, AnimalImageOut, AnimalLocationIn, AnimalLocationOut, AnimalOut, GalleryAnimalOut, LoginIn, MapLocationOut, UserCreate, UserOut

app = FastAPI(title="CowsIA Ganaderia API")
WORKSPACE_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
LOCAL_XML_PATH = WORKSPACE_ROOT / "LibroRegistroAnimaleGanaderoAFechas_10-07-2023_10-07-2026.xml"
UPLOAD_ROOT = WORKSPACE_ROOT / "backend" / "uploads"
ALLOWED_IMAGE_TYPES = {"image/jpeg": ".jpg", "image/png": ".png", "image/webp": ".webp", "image/gif": ".gif"}

app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=[
        "http://localhost:5173",
        "http://127.0.0.1:5173",
        "http://localhost:5174",
        "http://127.0.0.1:5174",
    ],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

PUBLIC_API_PATHS = {"/api/health", "/api/auth/login", "/api/auth/session", "/api/auth/logout"}


@app.middleware("http")
async def require_authentication(request: Request, call_next):
    if request.method == "OPTIONS" or not request.url.path.startswith("/api/") or request.url.path in PUBLIC_API_PATHS:
        return await call_next(request)
    try:
        settings = get_auth_settings()
    except AuthConfigurationError as exc:
        return JSONResponse(status_code=503, content={"detail": str(exc)})
    user_id = request_user_id(request, settings)
    if not user_id:
        return JSONResponse(status_code=401, content={"detail": "Debes iniciar sesion"})
    with SessionLocal() as db:
        user = db.get(User, user_id)
        if not user or not user.active:
            return JSONResponse(status_code=401, content={"detail": "Debes iniciar sesion"})
        request.state.user_id = user.id
        request.state.farm_id = user.farm_id
        request.state.can_manage_users = bool(user.can_manage_users)
    return await call_next(request)


def current_farm_id(request: Request) -> int:
    return int(request.state.farm_id)


def require_user_manager(request: Request) -> None:
    if not request.state.can_manage_users:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Solo el administrador puede gestionar usuarios")


@app.on_event("startup")
def on_startup() -> None:
    init_db()
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        manager = db.query(User).filter(User.can_manage_users.is_(True), User.active.is_(True)).first()
        if manager:
            import_local_xml_if_empty(db, LOCAL_XML_PATH, manager.farm_id)
    finally:
        db.close()


@app.get("/api/health")
def health() -> dict[str, str]:
    return {"status": "ok"}


@app.post("/api/auth/login")
def login(payload: LoginIn, response: Response) -> dict[str, str | bool]:
    try:
        settings = get_auth_settings()
    except AuthConfigurationError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=str(exc)) from exc
    with SessionLocal() as db:
        user = db.query(User).filter(User.username == payload.username.strip(), User.active.is_(True)).first()
        if not user or not password_is_valid(payload.password, user.password_hash):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Usuario o contrasena incorrectos")
        farm = db.get(Farm, user.farm_id)
        user_id = user.id
        username = user.username
        farm_name = farm.name if farm else ""
        can_manage_users = bool(user.can_manage_users)
    response.set_cookie(
        key=SESSION_COOKIE_NAME,
        value=create_session_token(user_id, settings),
        max_age=settings.session_seconds,
        httponly=True,
        secure=settings.cookie_secure,
        samesite="strict",
        path="/api",
    )
    response.headers["Cache-Control"] = "no-store"
    return {
        "authenticated": True,
        "username": username,
        "farm_name": farm_name,
        "can_manage_users": can_manage_users,
    }


@app.get("/api/auth/session")
def auth_session(request: Request) -> dict[str, str | bool | None]:
    try:
        settings = get_auth_settings()
    except AuthConfigurationError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=str(exc)) from exc
    user_id = request_user_id(request, settings)
    if not user_id:
        return {"authenticated": False, "username": None, "farm_name": None, "can_manage_users": False}
    with SessionLocal() as db:
        user = db.get(User, user_id)
        if not user or not user.active:
            return {"authenticated": False, "username": None, "farm_name": None, "can_manage_users": False}
        farm = db.get(Farm, user.farm_id)
        return {
            "authenticated": True,
            "username": user.username,
            "farm_name": farm.name if farm else None,
            "can_manage_users": bool(user.can_manage_users),
        }


@app.post("/api/auth/logout")
def logout(response: Response) -> dict[str, bool]:
    response.delete_cookie(SESSION_COOKIE_NAME, path="/api")
    response.headers["Cache-Control"] = "no-store"
    return {"authenticated": False}


@app.get("/api/admin/users", response_model=list[UserOut])
def list_users(_: None = Depends(require_user_manager), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> list[dict]:
    rows = db.query(User, Farm).join(Farm, User.farm_id == Farm.id).order_by(User.username.asc()).all()
    return [
        {
            "id": user.id,
            "username": user.username,
            "farm_name": farm.name,
            "can_manage_users": bool(user.can_manage_users),
            "active": bool(user.active),
        }
        for user, farm in rows
    ]


@app.post("/api/admin/users", response_model=UserOut)
def create_user(payload: UserCreate, _: None = Depends(require_user_manager), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict:
    username = payload.username.strip()
    farm_name = payload.farm_name.strip()
    if len(username) < 3:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="El usuario debe tener al menos 3 caracteres")
    if len(payload.password) < 10:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="La contrasena debe tener al menos 10 caracteres")
    if not farm_name:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="La explotacion es obligatoria")
    if db.query(User).filter(User.username == username).first():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="El usuario ya existe")
    farm = db.query(Farm).filter(Farm.name == farm_name).first()
    if not farm:
        farm = Farm(name=farm_name)
        db.add(farm)
        db.flush()
    user = User(
        username=username,
        password_hash=hash_password(payload.password),
        farm_id=farm.id,
        can_manage_users=False,
        active=True,
    )
    db.add(user)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(user)
    return {
        "id": user.id,
        "username": user.username,
        "farm_name": farm.name,
        "can_manage_users": False,
        "active": True,
    }

async def create_animal_event_with_image(
    crotal: str,

    reporter: str | None = Form(None),
    file: UploadFile | None = File(None),

    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
) -> dict:

    image_name = None
    image_content_type = None
    if file and file.filename:
        suffix = _image_suffix(file)
        content = await file.read()
        target = _store_upload("event_images", file, content, suffix)

        text=clean_text,
        event_date=event_date,
        reporter=reporter,
        image_path=image_path,
        image_name=image_name,
        image_content_type=image_content_type,
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(row)
    return _event_out(row)


@app.get("/api/animal-events/{event_id}/image")
def get_animal_event_image(event_id: int, farm_id: int = Depends(current_farm_id), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> FileResponse:
    row = _owned_child(db, AnimalEvent, event_id, farm_id)
    if not row.image_path:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Imagen no encontrada")
    path = _resolve_upload_path(row.image_path, "event_images")
    if not path.exists():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Imagen no encontrada en disco")
    return FileResponse(path, filename=row.image_name or path.name, media_type=row.image_content_type)


@app.delete("/api/animal-events/{event_id}")
def delete_animal_event(event_id: int, farm_id: int = Depends(current_farm_id), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, bool]:
    row = _owned_child(db, AnimalEvent, event_id, farm_id)
    if row.image_path:
        path = _resolve_upload_path(row.image_path, "event_images")
        shared_file = db.query(AnimalEvent).filter(AnimalEvent.id != row.id, AnimalEvent.image_path == row.image_path).first()
        if path.exists() and not shared_file:
            path.unlink()
    db.delete(row)
    db.commit()
    return {"ok": True}

const API_BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE ?? "";

async function request(path, options = {}) {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${path}`, { credentials: "include", ...options });
  if (!response.ok) {
    const error = await response.json().catch(() => ({ detail: response.statusText }));
    if (response.status === 401 && !path.startsWith("/api/auth/")) {
      window.dispatchEvent(new Event("cowsia:auth-required"));
    }
    throw new Error(error.detail || "Error de API");
  }
  return response.json();
}

export const api = {
  get: (path) => request(path),
  post: (path, body = {}) =>
    request(path, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
    }),
  upload: (path, file) => {
    const form = new FormData();
    form.append("file", file);
    return request(path, { method: "POST", body: form });
  },
  uploadForm: (path, form) => request(path, { method: "POST", body: form }),
  delete: (path) => request(path, { method: "DELETE" }),
  login: (username, password) =>
    request("/api/auth/login", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ username, password }),
    }),
  logout: () => request("/api/auth/logout", { method: "POST" }),
  session: () => request("/api/auth/session"),
  url: (path) => `${API_BASE}${path}`,
};

def _animal_by_crotal(db: Session, crotal: str, farm_id: int) -> Animal:
    animal = db.query(Animal).filter(Animal.farm_id == farm_id, Animal.crotal == crotal).first()
    if not animal:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Animal no encontrado")
    return animal


def _owned_child(db: Session, model, row_id: int, farm_id: int):
    row = (
        db.query(model)
        .join(Animal, model.animal_id == Animal.id)
        .filter(model.id == row_id, Animal.farm_id == farm_id)
        .first()
    )
    if not row:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Registro no encontrado")
    return row


def _image_suffix(file: UploadFile) -> str:
    suffix = ALLOWED_IMAGE_TYPES.get(file.content_type or "")
    if not suffix:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="Solo se admiten imagenes JPG, PNG, WEBP o GIF")
    return suffix


def _store_upload(folder: str, file: UploadFile, content: bytes, suffix: str) -> Path:
    target_dir = UPLOAD_ROOT / folder
    target_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    target = target_dir / f"{uuid4().hex}{suffix}"
    target.write_bytes(content)
    return target


def _portable_upload_path(path: Path) -> str:
    return path.relative_to(WORKSPACE_ROOT).as_posix()


def _resolve_upload_path(stored_path: str, folder: str) -> Path:
    path = Path(stored_path)
    candidates = [path]
    if not path.is_absolute():
        candidates.append(WORKSPACE_ROOT / path)
    stored_name = PureWindowsPath(stored_path).name
    if stored_name:
        candidates.append(UPLOAD_ROOT / folder / stored_name)
    return next((candidate for candidate in candidates if candidate.is_file()), candidates[-1])


def _image_out(row: AnimalImage) -> dict:
    return {
        "id": row.id,
        "animal_id": row.animal_id,
        "file_name": row.file_name,
        "content_type": row.content_type,
        "is_primary": bool(row.is_primary),
        "created_at": row.created_at,
        "image_url": f"/api/animal-images/{row.id}/file",
    }


def _event_out(row: AnimalEvent) -> dict:
    return {
        "id": row.id,
        "animal_id": row.animal_id,
        "text": row.text,
        "event_date": row.event_date,
        "reporter": row.reporter,
        "image_name": row.image_name,
        "created_at": row.created_at,
        "image_url": f"/api/animal-events/{row.id}/image" if row.image_path else None,
    }


def _age_years(born: date | None) -> float | None:
    if not born:
        return None
    return round((date.today() - born).days / 365.25, 1)


@app.get("/api/animals/{crotal}/details", response_model=AnimalDetailOut)
def animal_details(crotal: str, farm_id: int = Depends(current_farm_id), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict:
    animal = _animal_by_crotal(db, crotal, farm_id)
    images = db.query(AnimalImage).filter(AnimalImage.animal_id == animal.id).order_by(AnimalImage.is_primary.desc(), AnimalImage.created_at.asc(), AnimalImage.id.asc()).all()
    locations = db.query(AnimalLocation).filter(AnimalLocation.animal_id == animal.id).order_by(AnimalLocation.recorded_at.desc()).all()
    events = db.query(AnimalEvent).filter(AnimalEvent.animal_id == animal.id).order_by(AnimalEvent.event_date.desc().nullslast(), AnimalEvent.created_at.desc()).all()
    return {
        "animal": animal,
        "images": [_image_out(row) for row in images],
        "locations": locations,
        "events": [_event_out(row) for row in events],
    }

function formatDate(value) {
  if (!value) return "";
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-ES").format(new Date(`${value}T00:00:00`));
}

function years(value) {
  if (!value) return "";
  const born = new Date(`${value}T00:00:00`);
  const diff = (Date.now() - born.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24 * 365.25);
  return `${diff.toFixed(1)} anos`;
}

function toQueryString(values) {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  Object.entries(values).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
      value.filter(Boolean).forEach((item) => params.append(key, item));
    } else if (value) {
      params.append(key, value);
    }
  });
  const suffix = params.toString();
  return suffix ? `?${suffix}` : "";
}

function AuthenticatedApp() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState({ loading: true, username: null });

  useEffect(() => {
    api.session()
      .then((result) => setSession({ loading: false, ...(result.authenticated ? result : { username: null }) }))
      .catch((error) => setSession({ loading: false, username: null, error: error.message }));

    function requireLogin() {
      setSession({ loading: false, username: null });
    }
    window.addEventListener("cowsia:auth-required", requireLogin);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("cowsia:auth-required", requireLogin);
  }, []);

  if (session.loading) {
    return <div className="auth-loading">Comprobando sesion...</div>;
  }
  if (!session.username) {
    return <Login initialError={session.error} onLogin={(result) => setSession({ loading: false, ...result })} />;
  }
  return (
    <App
      username={session.username}
      farmName={session.farm_name}
      canManageUsers={session.can_manage_users}
      onLogout={async () => {
        try {
          await api.logout();
        } finally {
          setSession({ loading: false, username: null });
        }
      }}
    />
  );
}

+    if longitude is not None and not -180 <= longitude <= 180:

+def get_farm_event_image(event_id: int, farm_id: int = Depends(current_farm_id), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> FileResponse:
+    row = db.query(FarmEvent).filter(FarmEvent.id == event_id, FarmEvent.farm_id == farm_id).first()
+    if not row or not row.image_path:
+        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Imagen no encontrada")

+    if not path.exists():
+        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Imagen no encontrada en disco")

+@app.delete("/api/farm-events/{event_id}")
+def delete_farm_event(event_id: int, farm_id: int = Depends(current_farm_id), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, bool]:

+        shared_farm_file = db.query(FarmEvent).filter(FarmEvent.id != row.id, FarmEvent.image_path == row.image_path).first()
+        if path.exists() and not shared_animal_file and not shared_farm_file:
+            path.unlink()
+    db.delete(row)
+    db.commit()
+    return {"ok": True}

+const incidentCategories = [

+function FarmIncidents() {
+  const [rows, setRows] = useState([]);
+  const [form, setForm] = useState({
+    category: incidentCategories[0],
+    text: "",
+    occurred_at: localDateTimeValue(),
+    latitude: "",
+    longitude: "",
+  });
+  const [imageFile, setImageFile] = useState(null);
+  const [fileKey, setFileKey] = useState(0);
+  const [locating, setLocating] = useState(false);
+  const [error, setError] = useState("");
+
+  async function load() {
+    setRows(await api.get("/api/farm-events"));
+  }
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    load().catch((exc) => setError(exc.message));
+  }, []);
+
+  function captureLocation() {
+    setError("");
+    if (!navigator.geolocation) {
+      setError("Este dispositivo no ofrece geolocalizacion.");
+      return;
+    }
+    setLocating(true);
+    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
+      (position) => {
+        setForm((current) => ({
+          ...current,
+          latitude: position.coords.latitude.toFixed(6),
+          longitude: position.coords.longitude.toFixed(6),
+        }));
+        setLocating(false);
+      },
+      (locationError) => {
+        setError(`No se pudo obtener la ubicacion: ${locationError.message}`);
+        setLocating(false);
+      },
+      { enableHighAccuracy: true, timeout: 12_000 },
+    );
+  }
+
+  async function submit(event) {
+    event.preventDefault();
+    setError("");
+    try {
+      const payload = new FormData();
+      payload.append("category", form.category);
+      payload.append("text", form.text);
+      if (form.occurred_at) payload.append("occurred_at", new Date(form.occurred_at).toISOString());
+      if (form.latitude && form.longitude) {
+        payload.append("latitude", form.latitude);
+        payload.append("longitude", form.longitude);
+      }
+      if (imageFile) payload.append("file", imageFile);
+      await api.uploadForm("/api/farm-events-with-image", payload);
+      setForm({ category: incidentCategories[0], text: "", occurred_at: localDateTimeValue(), latitude: "", longitude: "" });
+      setImageFile(null);
+      setFileKey((value) => value + 1);
+      await load();
+    } catch (exc) {
+      setError(exc.message);
+    }
+  }
+
+  async function remove(row) {
+    setError("");
+    try {
+      await api.delete(`/api/farm-events/${row.id}`);
+      await load();
+    } catch (exc) {
+      setError(exc.message);
+    }
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <section className="page incidents-page">
+      <PageTitle title="Incidencias de la explotacion" />
+      <div className="grid two incidents-layout">
+        <form className="panel incident-form" onSubmit={submit}>
+          <h2>Nueva incidencia</h2>
+          <p className="muted">El usuario que ha iniciado sesion se guarda automaticamente como informante.</p>
+          <label>
+            Tipo
+            <select value={form.category} onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, category: event.target.value })}>
+              {incidentCategories.map((category) => <option key={category}>{category}</option>)}
+            </select>
+          </label>
+          <label>
+            Descripcion
+            <textarea required placeholder="Describe lo sucedido" value={form.text} onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, text: event.target.value })} />
+          </label>
+          <label>
+            Fecha y hora
+            <input required type="datetime-local" value={form.occurred_at} onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, occurred_at: event.target.value })} />
+          </label>
+          <div className="incident-location">
+            <input aria-label="Latitud" type="number" step="0.000001" placeholder="Latitud" value={form.latitude} onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, latitude: event.target.value })} />
+            <input aria-label="Longitud" type="number" step="0.000001" placeholder="Longitud" value={form.longitude} onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, longitude: event.target.value })} />
+            <button type="button" onClick={captureLocation} disabled={locating}><MapPin size={16} />{locating ? "Localizando..." : "Usar mi ubicacion"}</button>
+          </div>
+          <label>
+            Fotografia opcional
+            <input key={fileKey} type="file" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp,image/gif" onChange={(event) => setImageFile(event.target.files?.[0] || null)} />
+          </label>
+          <button className="primary" type="submit"><Plus size={16} />Guardar incidencia</button>
+          {error && <p className="error">{error}</p>}
+        </form>
+        <div className="incident-history">
+          <div className="result-strip"><ClipboardList size={18} /><strong>{rows.length}</strong><span>incidencias registradas</span></div>
+          {rows.length === 0 && <div className="panel"><p className="muted">Todavia no hay incidencias generales.</p></div>}
+          {rows.map((row) => (
+            <article className="panel incident-card" key={row.id}>

+              <div className="incident-meta"><span><CalendarPlus size={15} />{formatDateTime(row.occurred_at)}</span><span>{row.reporter}</span></div>
+              {row.latitude !== null && row.longitude !== null && <a className="incident-map-link" href={`https://www.google.com/maps?q=${row.latitude},${row.longitude}`} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer"><MapPin size={15} />{row.latitude}, {row.longitude}<ExternalLink size={14} /></a>}
+              {row.image_url && <img src={api.url(row.image_url)} alt={row.image_name || row.category} />}
+            </article>
